# Orphica, nova editio accurata in usum praelectionum academicarum et scholarum

- **Title:** Orphica, nova editio accurata in usum praelectionum academicarum et scholarum
- **Author:** Orpheus (attributed); edited by Gottfried Heinrich Schäfer
- **Edition:** Leipzig: Weigel, 1818; Internet Archive item orphicanovaediti00schf (University of Toronto). A school edition of the Greek hymns, Argonautica and Lithica.
- **Translator:** none
- **Year:** 1818
- **Read from:** https://archive.org/download/orphicanovaediti00schf/orphicanovaediti00schf_djvu.txt
- **Licence / copyright status:** Public domain (published 1818).
